matrix.tows@1

Internal rows (strength, weakness) crossed with external columns (opportunity, threat); cells are strategy notes.

Profile verification

  • Explicit ordered row/column references and one named relation role; direction is row→column.
  • Cells retain every contributing relationship ID and endpoint reference. Duplicate assignments reject unless explicitly join-enabled.
  • At most 5000 cells and 40 columns; a blank cell is missing, not an implied relationship.
  • Exactly two rows and two columns with x_category axis/level internal:strength/weakness by external:opportunity/threat. Preserve declared order; cells are declared strategy content, not inferred recommendations.

Profile defaults

Explicit file settings override eligible CSS, then these profile defaults and the common standard apply. Unspecified semantic facts remain unspecified.

{
  "projection.kind": "matrix",
  "projection.profile": "matrix.tows@1",
  "projection.duplicates": "join"
}

Projection properties

kind, profile, width, height, write_data, rows, columns, relation, value, duplicates, encoding

Search the property codex

Design operations

  • Select/reorder row and column axes; create/edit/delete the source assignment for a cell; choose scalar value binding and duplicate policy.
Bounded analysis

Deterministic assignment projection and profile-specific cell/domain/completion checks; no implied organizational authority or strategy optimizer.

Outside this profile

  • SWOT inference
  • strategy recommendation
